*   3 jaw universal oil filter wrench
*   Remove all passenger car and light - duty Truck spin - on - type filters with this wrench
*   Sizes range from 2-1/2 to 3-3/4" (63.5 to 95mm)
*   Use with a 3/8" Square drive tool
*   Designed to deliver trouble-free, consistent performance
*   Right for your lifestyle

[How customer reviews and ratings work](javascript:void(0))

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

[Learn more how customers reviews work on Amazon](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_omni_lm_btn?nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)

### Customers say

Customers find the wrench easy to use, particularly in tight spaces, and appreciate its compact size and ability to fit in tight areas. The functionality is positive, with one customer noting it works well in hard-to-reach areas. While some customers report good grip on the filter, others mention the fingers aren't long enough to catch it properly. Customers disagree on the value for money, with some finding it good value while others consider it expensive. The filter tightening aspect receives mixed feedback, with several customers reporting they can't tighten the new filter with it.
